# Triage: notes still owed to docs/debts/notes.md on main

Checked Nov 2 12:58 CST: none of these is in docs/debts/notes.md on main. Triage writes only the roadmap branch, so the next lane that edits notes.md lands them.

Sorted 14:39 CST Nov 16 (triage; a read-only Explore agent, sonnet, read the headed sections at lines 649-739 only; nothing in this file is marked moved). Debt and note drafts not on main (11 slugs checked against origin/main notes.md and docs/debts/): the sections headed 2026-11-10 20:15, 2026-11-10 20:17, 2026-11-10 23:35, 2026-11-11 11:07 and 2026-11-11 12:53. Tasks, not notes: the sections headed 2026-11-11 11:14 and 2026-11-11 11:34. Mixed: the section headed 2026-11-11 13:59 (lines 730-735 are tasks, 737-739 are notes). Not sorted: lines 5-648 and 740 to the end. Reader for the sorted notes: the lane that lands the notes slice after draft PR #3569 (one file per new note) is decided; slice A's first record is draft PR #3577. Form of a note on main: one line, `- <slug up to 32 characters>: <finding> (<file or symbol>). Decision: <slug>. Fix: <fix or none>. Until: <date>`.

- code-check-exclude-unchecked (c127, Oct 28 09:53 CDT, from banchi #993's review): the rule that a repo's code-check exclude holds only vendored or generated files lives only as a comment in check.yml. A debt after a real incident.
- qmax-tsc-pin-drift (c127, Oct 28 09:55 CDT): q_max pins tsc 5.8.3 while actions/code-check uses 5.9.3; they differ on one TS2322 case. A debt if they disagree on a PR.
- code-check-exclude-fork-point (c182, PR #777 round 2): the exclude list is compared against main's entries plus the fork point's, so a branch can re-add an entry main removed after the fork.
- code-check-precommit-rename (c182): in a pre-commit run, an unstaged new file makes a rename read as a plain delete; CI is not affected.
- code-check-unknown-main-noise (c182): without --main, every run prints "unknown: main"; noise only.
- debt-branch-pointer (triage, Nov 2 13:02 CST): docs/debts/agent-reports-with-live-watcher.md names branch stop-blocks-live-tasks; once page item 170's deletes run, that branch lives only as archive/stop-blocks-live-tasks (same tip b8feeb52). Re-point the debt in the same edit.

- classifier-refuses-merge-reviewed (runner 9, q_max #1106 lane, Nov 7 23:31 CST; coordinator 23:30 CST): the auto-mode classifier refused `merge --reviewed`, a read-only check, as "Merge Without Review". A likely fix is an autoMode.allow entry for `merge --reviewed`, `merge --lane-lint` and `merge --review-record`; the owner pastes such entries himself (notes line classifier-refuses-allow-brief, item 257 c). Held unless it recurs. Decision: refusals-reach-the-owner.
- Lane batch63-lint-a, #1420 (verdict and ci-job start with an empty stdin) round-1 notes, to docs/debts/notes.md (coordinator, Nov 7 23:38 CST): (a) nowindow-stdin-inherits: hooks/_nowindow.py: with stdin set, a Windows child with no redirect inherits the caller's stdout and stderr, so a future hook call without capture_output would write into the hook's JSON stdout. Fix: capture or redirect in the one helper. (b) stamp-node-console-stdin-mechanism: the notes line stamp-node-console-stdin misstates its mechanism; stamp.mjs runs `bash -c` through execFileSync, not a Node child. Fix: reword the line. (c) lint-past-bash-cap-pin: docs/debts/lint-past-bash-cap.md says lint/test_ci_job.py pins the lint cost; no such pin exists. Fix: add the pin or cut the claim. (d) verdict-stdin-windows-doc: lint/test_verdict.py StdinPassesThrough skips Windows, and the verdict header does not say a Windows command gets an empty stdin. Fix: say it in the header.
- Lane revive-1042 (#1042), to docs/debts/notes.md (coordinator, Nov 7 23:39 CST): (a) shell-write-quote-split-interpreter: `_shell_could_write` misses a quote-split interpreter word such as `pyth""on3`. Fix: none — attacker-only (guard-guards-mistakes-only). (b) sed-w-needs-space: the sed `w` check needs a space after `w`, so `w/path` passes; a gap that predates #1042. - Lane review-rounds-rework (#1789, #1773), to docs/debts/notes.md (coordinator, Nov 7 23:41 CST); the lane proposed no fixes, so each Fix line is to be written by the notes lane from the PR: (a) record-check-amend-force-push: a lane's amend plus force-push escapes the review-record check. - replacement-pr-carries-review review notes (lane replacement-pr-carries-review, session_013NEJL1WFQtTFeWPY7r1qYP, #2207, rounds 1-3 PASS, net value positive; Nov 8 13:40 CST), none a debt yet:
  1. A plain --fix-base-record stand-in counts one link per PR, so source chain 1 + stand-in + one same-PR fix gives 3 fix checks across a lineage (per-PR count kept under owner item 426 a, 11:03 CST).
  2. source_chain_problem lists the repo's comments since the source PR opened; a long-lived source pages many lists. A per-issue read would be cheaper.
  3. A carry does not compare the source PR's base branch with the new PR's; a same-tree head on a retargeted base merges a different diff than reviewed. The plain stand-in has the same gap.
  4. docs/decisions/pr-review-record.md Cost bullet omits the plain stand-in's one source PR read.
  5. `merge --reviewed` counts plain links with no tree reads, so it reads a carry chain as one link where the write step counts the cap.
  6. The body match needs "replaces #N"; "replaces PR #N" is refused (safe; the remedy names the form).
  7. A notes-only commit on the replacement breaks tree equality, so it gets full rounds where the same-PR path accepts notes diffs.

  - message-ref-link-text-slow: MESSAGE_LINK_TEXT (hooks/guard.py) is quadratic on a line with many `[` and no `]`: 7.8 s at 32,000 characters; message_refs takes 40 s on a 64 KiB line. A hook timeout fails open.
  - message-ref-link-forms: `[PR #8](<https://github.com/other/repo/pull/8>)` and a reference-style link `[PR #8][1]` to another repo are still judged against this repo.

Quoted data is not an act. One sighting; no open step owns the check.
- (debt) reviewed-chain-hostless-count (lane replacement-pr-carries-review, #2344 round 1 finding 2, reviewer haiku, Decision: pr-review-record): `merge --reviewed` (merge/merge.py, reviewed_read) counts the fix chain with no host, so a carried record counts as one link, not the full cap. Probe: [carry, hand-posted fix] counts 2 and is not flagged, while the write step refuses that fix. Reached only by a record posted outside the tool; three such hand posts on Nov 8 (step record-tool-posts-itself).
- (debt) carry-base-mismatch (lane replacement-pr-carries-review, #2344 round 3, reviewer sonnet, Decision: pr-review-record): carry_problem (merge/merge.py 3741-3756) never compares the source PR's base with this PR's base. A stacked on branch S, replacement B opened against main at the same tree while S is open, carries A's PASS, so S's content reaches main past S's review. Harm moderate, odds low. A strict base-equal check would refuse a legit retarget after S merged, so the fix needs a design choice.
- (debt) plain-copy-fail-remedy (same source): merge/merge.py 3975, a plain copy of a FAIL below round 3 prints the round-3 remedy; it should reuse source_record_problem's round-1 remedy.
- (debt) wide-plain-copy-unpinned (same source): no test pins a WIDE plain copy, so the mutant `!= "PASS"` to `== "FAIL"` at merge/merge.py 3974 survives.
- equal-tree-stand-in-full-cap (same source): an equal-tree stand-in counts the full cap even when its source has no Fix line (merge/merge.py 3492-3494); it fails closed.
- source-chain-dead-non-carry (same source): source_chain_problem is dead for a non-carry source (merge/merge.py 4003).
- carry-cost-line-short (same source): decision pr-review-record's Cost line omits the source-PR read and the notes-step tree reads.

- gh-write-ansi-c-quote (same source): shlex fails open on `$'..'` bodies. Fix: on a shlex error in a gh command, refuse (fail closed) in cloud.

- cross-repo-question-reason (lane cqch, #2229, via coordinator 11:40 CST Nov 9): the last bullet of "The ruling" in docs/decisions/cross-repo-question-checked.md says the refusal reason names the page's own repo, but guard.py prints the generic PAGE_REASONS text and `matched` goes only to guard.log. Fix: show `matched` in the reason, or correct the sentence.
- mutate-crash-reads-missing (same source): a crashed suite's stderr (ModuleNotFoundError) never reaches run_mutant's returned lines, so a crash reads only as a missing required case. Fix: carry the crash's stderr tail into the mutant's result line.
- silent-undo-rewrap-reads-drop (lane notes-fix-large-1, #1486 head c94378eb7, a merge of main 81a720c42, 12:16 CST Nov 9): lint/check_silent_undo.py compares whole lines, so a merge that re-wraps a `from guard_core import (...)` list in hooks/guard_frozen.py to add _walk_wrappers and LOOP_KEYWORDS reads as dropping 1 line main added, though every name main added is still there. The lane uses the Drops-lines trailer. Fix: for a dropped line inside a bracketed import list, compare the names in the list, not the lines. Becomes a debt on a second sighting.
- question-form-widen-by-meaning (triage, 12:27 CST Nov 9): page item 464 v1 went up with a widening option (patch the platform's shared Stop-hook script) recommended and unflagged; the coordinator caught it and v2 fixed it. hooks/question_form.py checks the label and pick only on an option already marked widens:true, so it cannot see a widening by meaning. Fix: question_form flags an option whose text names patching, forking or an exception to a shared or platform file, and asks the writer to mark widens or say why not.

- resolver-case-paren-restores-table (lane resolve-vars-quote-split, #2374, 13:25 CST Nov 9; decision frozen-path-resolves-shell-vars; until 2026-11-23): in hooks/guard_frozen.py `_resolve_into` (about lines 596-602), a case-pattern `)` inside a `( ... )` group pops the nearest `(` and restores the table early, so an earlier assignment in the group is forgotten. `( D=T; case x in a) echo hi;; esac; echo y > $D )` is refused on main and allowed on #2374's head; real bash writes T. Rare: it needs a frozen assignment and a case in one group. File after #2374 merges.
- resolver-unset-f-clears (same source): `D=T; unset -f D; echo y > $D` is refused on main and allowed on #2374's head, because the resolver treats `unset -f D` as clearing the variable. `unset -f` removes a function, not the variable. Claude does not write it. File after #2374 merges.

- hooks-lag-shallow-count (lane live-hooks-lag-named, #2473): after a depth-1 mirror refresh plus a partial failure, the lag line can read "1 commits behind" while the true count is larger, because history is cut. Fix: say "at least N" when the history is shallow.

Test nits: no case for a record naming two sessions, none for a named row inside `retagged`. Fix: refuse or ask on a live named row whatever its tags.

- buckets-read-merging-checkout-caps (#2591 round 3, sonnet, comment 6804487229): merge/merge.py Host.buckets (1260-1268) reads job caps from the merging checkout. After a cap raise, main's older cap-cut push runs bucket as a plain cancel, which is UNKNOWN at 1499-1503, so `red_base` (3185) refuses the next merge until a newer push run passes. The way out is `--base-red-ok` or a checkout on main. A false alarm, likely one-time per cap change.
- round-two-skip-misread (triage, 12:45 CST): a round 1 reviewer wrote "Round 2 skips" on a guard change. The merge tool needs rounds 1-3 in order for a guard or check change, so the record read "open". Briefs should say that a guard change always runs 3 rounds.

- (debt) fix-check-refuses-after-pass (coordinator 14:31 CST Nov 15, relayed; each gap is the lane's report, not measured by the coordinator or triage; likely in normal work: yes): What: `merge --review-record --fix-check` refuses when the newest earlier record on the PR already has a PASS fix check and the new edits are not a main merge (lane batch-runner-rolling-train, #3341 and #3357), so a lane that fixes after a passing fix check cannot record honestly except by a fresh round 1. Where: unverified (the record tool's fix-check path; not read by triage). Decision: unverified. Third case (coordinator 14:59 and 15:00 CST Nov 15, lane-read, unverified by triage): #3505 (lane unknown-read-lint-flake). Cases so far #3341, #3357, #3505, all docs-only (n=3 PRs hit; time cost unmeasured, an earlier "15 min of fixes" figure was withdrawn). Verbatim tool refusals the lane quoted: (a) "--fix-check: the reviewed head must be the Head of the newest earlier review log on this pull request, and that log must have no Fix check line or a PASS one. After a PASS one, a head that is a merge must be a merge of the base into that Head, and the log must read done, with no edit beyond git's merge" and (b) for a fresh round 1: "this record restarts the review: it has the same or fewer rounds than an earlier record on this pull request, stops below round 3, and has no fix verifier ... A new branch with a new PR is the last resort." The two refusals form a loop.
